\displaystyle=\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}^{2}(\ssomega)\Big[\mathfrak{a}_{\gamma m}h^{m}+\mathds{1}(0\leq m\leq 2\gamma)\mathfrak{b}_{\gamma m}h^{2\gamma-m}\Big].

Putting all together yields that

\displaystyle(d{\swabfamily M}_{-}(\xi)[h])^{m}

\displaystyle=-\left(d\mathfrak{A}\xi[h]\right)^{m}+\left(d\langle\mathfrak{f}^{(3)}\rangle(\xi)[h]\right)^{m}+\left(d\mathfrak{F}_{0}(\xi)[h]\right)^{m}

is given by

\displaystyle-h^{m}\left[\ssomega_{m}^{2}+\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}^{2}(\omega)\left(\frac{3}{4}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}-\mathfrak{a}_{\gamma m}\right)\right]-\mathds{1}(0\leq m\leq 2\gamma)h^{2\gamma-m}\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}^{2}(\omega)\left[\frac{3}{8}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma,2\gamma-m,\gamma,m}-\mathfrak{b}_{\gamma m}\right].

Finally, we rewrite this as follows

\displaystyle-\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}^{-2}(d{\swabfamily M}_{-}(\xi)[h])^{m}=\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}h^{m}+\mathds{1}(0\leq m\leq 2\gamma)\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma m}h^{2\gamma-m},

where we set

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}:=\left(\frac{\ssomega_{m}}{\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+\frac{3}{4}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}-\mathfrak{a}_{\gamma m},\hskip 5.0pt\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma m}:=\frac{3}{8}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma,2\gamma-m,\gamma,m}-\mathfrak{b}_{\gamma m}.

or equivalently as

\displaystyle-\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}^{-2}(d{\swabfamily M}_{-}(\xi)[h])^{m}

\displaystyle=\mathds{1}(0\leq m\leq\gamma-1)\Bigg[h^{m}\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}+h^{2\gamma-m}\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma m}\Bigg]+\mathds{1}(m=\gamma)\Bigg[h^{\gamma}\left(\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma\gamma}+\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma\gamma})\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathds{1}(\gamma+1\leq m\leq 2\gamma)\Bigg[h^{m}\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}+h^{2\gamma-m}\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma m}\Bigg]+\mathds{1}(m\geq 2\gamma+1)\Bigg[h^{m}\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}\Bigg],

that completes the proof.
∎

7.
Non–degeneracy conditions for the 1–modes

In this section, we derive and establish the crucial non–degeneracy conditions for 1–mode initial data according to Theorem
2.4
(for CW and CH) and Theorem
2.5
(for YM).

7.1.
Conformal cubic wave equation in spherical symmetry

Firstly, we consider the conformal cubic wave equation in spherical symmetry and derive of the non-degeneracy condition for the 1–modes.

Lemma 7.1

(CW model: Derivation of the non-degeneracy condition for the 1–modes)
.

Let
\gamma\geq 0
be any integer and define
\xi
to be the rescaled 1–mode according to (
6.1
)–(
6.2
). Then, the non–degeneracy condition

\displaystyle\ker\left(d\mathcal{M}(\xi)\right)=\{0\}

is equivalent to

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\omega_{m}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma mm}\neq 0,\text{ for all }m\geq 2\gamma+1,\\
D_{\gamma n}\neq 0,\text{ for all }n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\},\end{cases}

(7.1)

where

\displaystyle D_{\gamma n}:=\left[\omega_{n}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma nn}\right]\left[\omega_{2\gamma-n}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma,\gamma,2\gamma-n,2\gamma-n}\right]-\left[\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma,2\gamma-n,\gamma,n}\right]^{2}.

Proof.

Let
\gamma\geq 0
be any integer and define
\xi
to be the rescaled 1–mode according to (
6.1
)–(
6.2
). Furthermore, pick any
h=\{h^{j}:j\geq 0\}\in l_{s+3}^{2}
such that
d\mathcal{M}(\xi)[h]=0
and fix any integer
m\geq 0
. Then, according to Lemma
6.2
, we have that the system

\displaystyle\left(\omega_{m}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma mm}\right)h^{m}-\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma,2\gamma-m,\gamma,m}h^{2\gamma-m}=0,\hskip 5.0pt\text{for }0\leq m\leq\gamma-1,

(7.2)

\displaystyle\left(\omega_{m}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma mm}\right)h^{m}-\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma,2\gamma-m,\gamma,m}h^{2\gamma-m}=0,\hskip 5.0pt\text{for }\gamma+1\leq m\leq 2\gamma,

(7.3)

\displaystyle\left(\omega_{m}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma mm}\right)h^{m}=0,\hskip 5.0pt\text{for }m\geq 2\gamma+1,

(7.4)

coupled to

\displaystyle\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}h^{\gamma}

\displaystyle=0,

(7.5)

has
h=\{h^{i}:i\geq 0\}=0
as the unique solution. Firstly, (
7.2
) yields
h^{\gamma}=0
due to the fact that
C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}\neq 0
and
\omega_{\gamma}\neq 0
for all
\gamma\geq 0
, whereas, for (
7.4
), we must have

\displaystyle\omega_{m}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma mm}\neq 0,

for all
m\geq 2\gamma+1
. Next, we rearrange (
7.2
) and (
7.3
) by setting
m=n
and
m=2\gamma-n
respectively and obtain

\displaystyle\left(\omega_{n}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma nn}\right)h^{n}-\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma,2\gamma-n,\gamma,n}h^{2\gamma-n}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle\left(\omega_{2\gamma-n}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma,\gamma,2\gamma-n,2\gamma-n}\right)h^{2\gamma-n}-\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma,n,\gamma,2\gamma-n}h^{n}

\displaystyle=0.

for all
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
, that can be written in the matrix form

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\omega_{n}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma nn}&-\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma,2\gamma-n,\gamma,n}\\
-\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma,n,\gamma,2\gamma-n}&\omega_{2\gamma-n}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma,\gamma,2\gamma-n,2\gamma-n}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}h^{n}\\
h^{2\gamma-n}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix},

for all
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
. Observe that these are in total
\gamma

(2\times 2)-
linear systems where the unknowns are
h^{m}
for
m\in\{0,1,\cdots,2\gamma\}\setminus\{\gamma\}
. Finally, these systems have only the trivial solution
h^{m}=0
for all
m\in\{0,1,\cdots,2\gamma\}\setminus\{\gamma\}
if and only if the determinants

\displaystyle D_{\gamma n}

\displaystyle=\left[\omega_{n}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma nn}\right]\left[\omega_{2\gamma-n}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma,\gamma,2\gamma-n,2\gamma-n}\right]-\left[\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma,2\gamma-n,\gamma,n}\right]^{2}

are non–zero for all
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
, that completes the proof.
∎

Next, we establish the non–degeneracy condition for this model.

Proposition 7.2

(Non-degeneracy condition for the 1–modes and the CW model)
.

Let
\gamma\geq 0
be any integer. Then, the non–degeneracy condition (
7.1
) holds true.

Proof.

Let
\gamma\geq 0
be any integer. Also, pick any integers
m\geq 2\gamma+1
and
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
. Then, according to Lemma
5.2
, we have

\displaystyle C_{ijkm}=\omega_{\min\{i,j,k,m\}}

provided that either

\displaystyle\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{m}=0,

\displaystyle\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{m}=0,

\displaystyle\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{m}=0.

One can easily show that all the indices
(i,j,k,m)
of the Fourier coefficients that appear in Lemma
7.1
satisfy at least one of these conditions and hence we infer

\displaystyle C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}

\displaystyle=\omega_{\gamma},

\displaystyle C_{\gamma\gamma nn}

\displaystyle=\omega_{n},

\displaystyle C_{\gamma\gamma mm}

\displaystyle=\omega_{\gamma},

\displaystyle C_{\gamma,\gamma,2\gamma-n,2\gamma-n}

\displaystyle=\omega_{\gamma},

\displaystyle C_{\gamma,2\gamma-n,\gamma,n}

\displaystyle=\omega_{n}.

Putting all together, yields

\displaystyle\omega_{m}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}C_{\gamma\gamma mm}=\omega_{\gamma}\left(\omega_{m}^{2}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}\right)\geq\omega_{\gamma}\left(\omega_{2\gamma+1}^{2}-2\omega_{\gamma}^{2}\right)=2\omega_{\gamma}^{3}\geq 2,

\displaystyle D_{\gamma n}=\omega_{n}\omega_{\gamma}^{2}(n-3-4\gamma)(n-\gamma)^{2}\neq 0,

for all
m\geq 2\gamma+1
and
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
, that competes the proof.
∎

7.2.
Conformal cubic wave equation out of spherical symmetry

Next, we consider the conformal cubic wave equation out of spherical symmetry and show that the non–degeneracy condition is a condition on the Fourier coefficients.

Lemma 7.3

(Derivation of the non-degeneracy condition for the 1–modes and the CH model)
.

Let
\gamma
and
\mu_{1},\mu_{2}
be any integers and define
\xi
according to (
6.3
)–(
6.4
). Then, the non–degeneracy condition

\displaystyle\ker\left(d\mathcal{M}(\xi)\right)=\{0\}

is equivalent to

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\left(\somega_{m}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}-2\left(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\neq 0,\text{ for all }m\geq 2\gamma+1,\\
\mathsf{D}_{\gamma n}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\neq 0,\text{ for all }n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\},\end{cases}

(7.6)

where

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}_{\gamma n}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}

\displaystyle:=\left[\left(\somega_{n}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}-2\left(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma nn}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\right]\cdot

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\big(\somega_{2\gamma-n}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\big)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}-2\left(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\gamma-n,2\gamma-n}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\right]-\left[\left(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma,2\gamma-n,\gamma,n}^{(\mu_{1},\mu_{2})}\right]^{2}.

Proof.

The proof is similar to the one of Lemma
7.1
.
∎

Next, we establish the non–degeneracy condition for this model.

Proposition 7.4

(Non-degeneracy condition for the 1–modes and the CH model)
.

Let
\gamma,\mu_{1},\mu_{2}\geq 0
be any integers with
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
and
\mu_{1}=\mu_{2}=:\mu
where
\mu
is either sufficiently small with
\mu\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
or sufficiently large. Then, the non–degeneracy condition (
7.6
) holds true.

Proof.

Let
\gamma,\mu_{1},\mu_{2}\geq 0
be any integers with
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
and
\mu_{1}=\mu_{2}=:\mu
where
\mu
is either sufficiently small with
\mu\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
or sufficiently large. Also, pick any integer
m\geq 2\gamma+1
and, according to Lemmata
5.4
and
5.5
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\lambda=0}^{\gamma}\mathsf{M}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\mathsf{M}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\xi_{\lambda}(\mu),

\displaystyle\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}^{(\mu,\mu)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\lambda=0}^{\gamma}\mathsf{M}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\mathsf{M}_{m}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\xi_{\lambda}(\mu),

where the function

\displaystyle\mathsf{M}_{m}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi^{3/2}}\frac{(4\lambda+4\mu+1)\Gamma\left(\lambda+\frac{1}{2}\right)\Gamma\left(2\mu+\frac{1}{2}\right)\Gamma\left(\lambda+\mu+\frac{1}{2}\right)}{\Gamma(\lambda+\mu+1)\Gamma(\lambda+2\mu+1)}\cdot

\displaystyle\cdot\frac{(2\mu+2m+1)\Gamma\left(m-\lambda+\frac{1}{2}\right)\Gamma(m+\lambda+2\mu+1)}{\Gamma(m-\lambda+1)\Gamma\left(m+\lambda+2\mu+\frac{3}{2}\right)}

is decreasing with respect to
m
. In addition, recall that the eigenvalues are given by

\displaystyle\left(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\right)^{2}:=(2m+1+2\mu)^{2}

and they are clearly increasing with respect to
m\geq 0
. In other words, the function

\displaystyle\mathsf{P}_{m}^{(\mu)}(\lambda):=\frac{\mathsf{M}_{m}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)}{\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}}

is decreasing with respect to
m
for all
m\geq\gamma
as a product of two positive and decreasing functions. In the following, we show that

\displaystyle\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}-2\left(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}^{(\mu,\mu)}

stays away from zero for all
m\geq 2\gamma+1
and
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
provided that
\mu
is either sufficiently small, say
\mu\in\{1,2,\dots,10\}
or sufficiently large. To this end, we use the monotonicity of
\mathsf{P}_{m}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)
with respect to
m
to infer

\displaystyle\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}-2\left(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}^{(\mu,\mu)}=

\displaystyle\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\big(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\left[\frac{\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}}{\big(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}}-2\frac{\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}^{(\mu,\mu)}}{\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}}\right]=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\big(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\left[\sum_{\lambda=0}^{\gamma}\frac{\mathsf{M}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)}{\big(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}}\mathsf{M}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\xi_{\lambda}(\mu)-2\sum_{\lambda=0}^{\gamma}\mathsf{M}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\frac{\mathsf{M}_{m}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)}{\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}}\xi_{\lambda}(\mu)\right]=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\big(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\left[\sum_{\lambda=0}^{\gamma}\mathsf{P}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\mathsf{M}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\xi_{\lambda}(\mu)-2\sum_{\lambda=0}^{\gamma}\mathsf{M}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\mathsf{P}_{m}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\xi_{\lambda}(\mu)\right]=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\big(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\sum_{\lambda=0}^{\gamma}\mathsf{M}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\left[\mathsf{P}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)-2\mathsf{P}_{m}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\right]\xi_{\lambda}(\mu)\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\big(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\sum_{\lambda=0}^{\gamma}\mathsf{M}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\left[\mathsf{P}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)-2\mathsf{P}_{2\gamma+1}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\right]\xi_{\lambda}(\mu)=\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\mathsf{S}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)},

where we set

\displaystyle\mathsf{S}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}:=\frac{1}{2}\big(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\sum_{\lambda=0}^{\gamma}\mathsf{M}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\left[\mathsf{P}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)-2\mathsf{P}_{2\gamma+1}^{(\mu)}(\lambda)\right]\xi_{\lambda}(\mu).

On the one hand, for all
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
and
\mu\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
we compute
\mathsf{S}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}
and verify that all
\mathsf{S}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}
are strictly positive. On the other hand, for all
\gamma\in\{0,1,\dots,10\}
and sufficiently large
\mu
, we firstly compute
\mathsf{S}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}
in terms of
\mu
and then derive its asymptotic expansion as
\mu\longrightarrow\infty
. For
\gamma=0
, we find

\displaystyle\mathsf{S}_{0}^{(\mu)}=\frac{4^{\mu}(2\mu+1)(10\mu+7)\Gamma\left(\mu+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}\Gamma\left(\mu+\frac{5}{2}\right)}{\pi(2\mu+3)^{2}\Gamma(\mu+1)\Gamma\left(2\mu+\frac{5}{2}\right)},

that is strictly positive, for all
\mu\geq 0
, and for
\gamma\in\{1,2,3,4,5\}
, we expand

\displaystyle\mathsf{S}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}=\sigma_{\gamma}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right),

as
\mu\longrightarrow\infty
, for some strictly positive constants
\sigma_{\gamma}
. Figure
4
illustrates the constants
\sigma_{\gamma}
as
\gamma
varies within
\{1,2,\dots,10\}
.

Figure 4.
The constants
\sigma_{\gamma}
as
\gamma
varies within
\{1,2,\dots,10\}
. They are all strictly positive.

Consequently, in both cases, we can ensure that
\mathsf{S}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}>0
and hence we conclude that

\displaystyle\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}-2\left(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}^{(\mu,\mu)}\geq\big(\somega_{m}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\mathsf{S}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}\geq\big(\somega_{2\gamma+1}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\mathsf{S}_{\gamma}^{(\mu)}>0,

for all
m\geq 2\gamma+1
. Finally, it remains to show that the determinants

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}_{\gamma n}^{(\mu,\mu)}

\displaystyle:=\left[\left(\somega_{n}^{(\mu,\mu)}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}-2\left(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma nn}^{(\mu,\mu)}\right]\cdot

\displaystyle\cdot\left[\big(\somega_{2\gamma-n}^{(\mu,\mu)}\big)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}-2\left(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\gamma-n,2\gamma-n}^{(\mu,\mu)}\right]-\left[\left(\somega_{\gamma}^{(\mu,\mu)}\right)^{2}\mathsf{C}_{\gamma,2\gamma-n,\gamma,n}^{(\mu,\mu)}\right]^{2}.

are all non–zero for
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
. To this end, for all
\gamma\in\{1,2,3,4,5\}
and
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
, we firstly compute each of the Fourier coefficients in the determinants above in terms of
\mu
and then either compute the determinants when
\mu
is sufficiently small
\mu\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
or compute their limit as
\mu\longrightarrow\infty
. For example, for
\gamma=1
, we have
n=0
and compute

\displaystyle\mathsf{D}_{10}^{(\mu,\mu)}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3\ 16^{\mu-1}}{\pi^{2}}(\mu+1)(2\mu+3)^{4}(2\mu+5)(4\mu+7)\cdot

\displaystyle\cdot\left(20\mu^{4}+328\mu^{3}+1029\mu^{2}+1155\mu+435\right)\frac{\Gamma\left(\mu+\frac{1}{2}\right)^{2}\Gamma\left(\mu+\frac{3}{2}\right)^{3}\Gamma\left(\mu+\frac{3}{2}\right)}{\Gamma(\mu+2)^{2}\Gamma\left(2\mu+\frac{9}{2}\right)^{2}}

and it is clearly strictly negative for all integers
\mu\geq 0
. For all
\gamma\in\{1,2,\dots,10\}
and
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
, we find either
\mathsf{D}_{\gamma n}^{(\mu,\mu)}<0
when
\mu
is sufficiently small and
\mathsf{D}_{\gamma n}^{(\mu,\mu)}\longrightarrow\pm\infty
when
\mu\longrightarrow\infty
, that completes the proof.
∎

7.3.
Yang–Mills equation in spherical symmetry

Finally, we consider the Yang–Mills equation in spherical symmetry and show that the non–degeneracy condition is a condition on the Fourier coefficients.

Lemma 7.5

(Derivation of the non-degeneracy condition for the 1–modes and the YM model)
.

Let
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
and define
\xi
according to (
6.5
)–(
6.6
). Then, the non–degeneracy condition

\displaystyle\ker\left(d{\swabfamily M}_{-}(\xi)\right)=\{0\}

is equivalent to

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma\gamma}+\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma\gamma}\neq 0,\\
\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}\neq 0,\text{ for all }m\geq 2\gamma+1,\\
\mathfrak{D}_{\gamma n}\neq 0,\text{ for all }n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\},\end{cases}

(7.7)

where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}_{\gamma n}

\displaystyle:=\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma n}\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma,2\gamma-n}-\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma n}\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma,2\gamma-n}

and

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle:=\left(\frac{\ssomega_{m}}{\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+\frac{3}{4}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\nu m}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}+\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}-\frac{9}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m\nu m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\nu}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\\
\nu\neq\pm(m-\gamma)-2\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m\gamma\nu}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle:=\frac{3}{8}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma,2\gamma-m,\gamma,m}-\frac{9}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{2\gamma-m,\nu,m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\nu}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(2\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\\
\nu\neq\pm(m-\gamma)-2\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\nu m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{2\gamma-m,\gamma,\nu}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{2\gamma-m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}.

Proof.

Let
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
and define
\xi
according to (
6.5
)–(
6.6
). Furthermore, assume that
\ker(d{\swabfamily M}_{-}(\xi))=\{0\}
and pick any
h=\{h^{j}:j\geq 0\}\in l_{s+3}^{2}
such that
d{\swabfamily M}_{-}(\xi)[h]=0
. Also, fix any integer
m\geq 0
. Then, according to Lemma
6.7
, we have that the system

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}h^{m}+\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma m}h^{2\gamma-m}=0,\hskip 5.0pt\text{ for }0\leq m\leq\gamma-1,

(7.8)

\displaystyle\left(\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma\gamma}+\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma\gamma}\right)h^{\gamma}=0,\hskip 5.0pt\text{ for }m=\gamma,

(7.9)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}h^{m}+\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma m}h^{2\gamma-m}=0,\hskip 5.0pt\text{ for }\gamma+1\leq m\leq 2\gamma,

(7.10)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}h^{m}=0,\hskip 5.0pt\text{ for }m\geq 2\gamma+1,

(7.11)

has
h=\{h^{i}:i\geq 0\}=0
as the unique solution, where

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle:=\left(\frac{\ssomega_{m}}{\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+\frac{3}{4}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}-\mathfrak{a}_{\gamma m},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle:=\frac{3}{8}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma,2\gamma-m,\gamma,m}-\mathfrak{b}_{\gamma m}

Furthermore,
\mathfrak{a}_{\gamma m}
and
\mathfrak{b}_{\gamma m}
are given by Lemma
2.15
and putting all together yields

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle:=\left(\frac{\ssomega_{m}}{\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+\frac{3}{4}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\nu m}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}+\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}\ssomega^{2}}-\frac{9}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m\nu m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\nu}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\\
\nu\neq\pm(m-\gamma)-2\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m\gamma\nu}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle:=\frac{3}{8}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma,2\gamma-m,\gamma,m}-\frac{9}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{2\gamma-m,\nu,m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\nu}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(2\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\\
\nu\neq\pm(m-\gamma)-2\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\nu m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{2\gamma-m,\gamma,\nu}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{2\gamma-m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}.

Now, (
7.9
) and (
7.11
) yield

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma\gamma}+\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma\gamma}\neq 0

and

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}\neq 0

for all
m\geq 2\gamma+1
. Next, we rearrange (
7.8
) and (
7.10
) by setting
m=n
and
m=2\gamma-n
respectively to obtain

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma n}h^{n}+\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma n}h^{2\gamma-n}=0,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma,2\gamma-n}h^{2\gamma-n}+\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma,2\gamma-n}h^{n}=0,

for all
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
, that can be written in the matrix form

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma n}&\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma n}\\
\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma,2\gamma-n}&\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma,2\gamma-n}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}h^{n}\\
h^{2\gamma-n}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\end{bmatrix},

for all
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
. Observe that these are in total
\gamma

(2\times 2)-
linear systems where the unknowns are
h^{m}
for
m\in\{0,1,\cdots,2\gamma\}\setminus\{\gamma\}
. Finally, these systems have only the trivial solution
h^{m}=0
for all
m\in\{0,1,\cdots,2\gamma\}\setminus\{\gamma\}
if and only if the determinants

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}_{\gamma n}

\displaystyle=\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma n}\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma,2\gamma-n}-\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma n}\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma,2\gamma-n}

are non–zero for all
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
, that completes the proof.
∎

Next, we establish the non–degeneracy condition for this model.

Proposition 7.6

(Non-degeneracy condition for the 1–modes and the YM model)
.

Let
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
. Then, the non–degeneracy condition (
7.7
) holds true.

Proof.

Let
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
,
\alpha=1/\sqrt{6}
and pick any frequency
\ssomega\in\mathcal{W}_{\alpha}
with
\ssomega<1
. Also, pick an integer
m\geq 2\gamma+1
and define

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle:=\left(\frac{\ssomega_{m}}{\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+\frac{3}{4}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\nu m}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}+\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}-\frac{9}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m\nu m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\nu}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\\
\nu\neq\pm(m-\gamma)-2\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m\gamma\nu}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle:=\frac{3}{8}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma,2\gamma-m,\gamma,m}-\frac{9}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{2\gamma-m,\nu,m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\nu}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(2\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\\
\nu\neq\pm(m-\gamma)-2\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\nu m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{2\gamma-m,\gamma,\nu}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{2\gamma-m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}.

Firstly, we show that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma\gamma}+\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma\gamma}\neq 0.

In this case, all the sums above are finite and in particular
\nu
varies within
\{0,1,\dots,2\gamma\}
. Hence, we compute
\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma\gamma}+\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma\gamma}
for all
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
and verify that its has a strictly positive lower bound. Figure
5

Figure 5.
The constants
\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma\gamma}+\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma\gamma}
as
\gamma
varies within
\{0,1,\dots,10\}
. They decrease and stay away from zero.

illustrates the constants
\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma\gamma}+\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma\gamma}
as
\gamma
varies within
\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
. Secondly, we show that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}\neq 0,

for
m\geq 2\gamma+1
. To this end, we note that
\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{ijm}=0
for all integers
i,j,m\geq 0
with either
m>i+j
or
|i-j|>m
(Lemma
5.8
). Specifically, for any integer
m\geq 2\gamma+1
, we focus on

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\nu m}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}+\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}},\hskip 5.0pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\\
\nu\neq\pm(m-\gamma)-2\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m\gamma\nu}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}

and note that we must have

\displaystyle m-\gamma\leq\nu\leq m+\gamma

since

•

m-\gamma=|m-\gamma|>\nu\Longrightarrow\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\nu m}=0
,

•

\nu>m+\gamma\Longrightarrow\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\nu m}=0
.

In addition, for all such
\nu
, the conditions
\nu\neq\pm(m-\gamma)-2
are satisfied since

•

\nu\geq m-\gamma\Longrightarrow(m-\gamma)-2<m-\gamma\leq\nu\Longrightarrow\nu\neq(m-\gamma)-2
,

•

m\geq 2\gamma+1\Longrightarrow-(m-\gamma)-2<0\Longrightarrow\nu\neq-(m-\gamma)-2
.

Consequently, for all
m\geq 2\gamma+1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle:=\left(\frac{\ssomega_{m}}{\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+\frac{3}{4}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=m-\gamma\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\nu m}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}+\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}-\frac{9}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m\nu m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\nu}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\nu=m-\gamma\end{subarray}}^{m+\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m\gamma\nu}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}

and by setting
\nu=\sigma+m-\gamma
and
\nu=\sigma
respectively we can rewrite the latter as follows

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle:=\left(\frac{\ssomega_{m}}{\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+\frac{3}{4}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\sigma=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\sigma+m-\gamma,m}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\sigma+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}+\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}-\frac{9}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\sigma=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m\sigma m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\sigma}}{\ssomega_{\sigma}^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\sigma=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m,\gamma,\sigma+m-\gamma}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\sigma+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}.

Now, recall from Lemma
6.6
that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}^{-2}

\displaystyle=\frac{3\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}-8\mathfrak{q}_{\gamma}}{-8\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}=-\frac{3}{8\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}+\frac{1}{\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\mathfrak{q}_{\gamma}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3}{8\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}+\frac{9}{4\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\sum_{\nu=0}^{2\gamma}\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{2}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}}+\frac{1}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(2\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}\right)

that yields

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\ssomega_{m}}{\mathfrak{K}_{\gamma}}\right)^{2}=-\frac{3\ssomega_{m}^{2}}{8\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}+\frac{9\ssomega_{m}^{2}}{4\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\sum_{\nu=0}^{2\gamma}\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{2}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}}+\frac{1}{\ssomega_{\nu}^{2}-(2\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}\right).

Putting all together, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3\ssomega_{m}^{2}}{8\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}+\frac{9\ssomega_{m}^{2}}{4\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\sum_{\sigma=0}^{2\gamma}\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\sigma}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{2}{\ssomega_{\sigma}^{2}}+\frac{1}{\ssomega_{\sigma}^{2}-(2\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\sigma=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\sigma+m-\gamma,m}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\sigma+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}+\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}-\frac{9}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\sigma=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m\sigma m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\sigma}}{\ssomega_{\sigma}^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\sigma=0\end{subarray}}^{2\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m,\gamma,\sigma+m-\gamma}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{\sigma+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}.

In addition, we also note that
\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{ijm}=0
for all integers
i,j,m\geq 0
with
i+j-m\notin 2\mathbb{N}\cup\{0\}
(Lemma
5.8
). Specifically, we must have

\displaystyle\sigma\in 2\mathbb{N}\cup\{0\}

since

•

\sigma=\sigma+\gamma-\gamma\notin 2\mathbb{N}\cup\{0\}\Longrightarrow\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma\gamma\sigma}=0
,

•

\sigma=\gamma+\sigma+m-\gamma-m\notin 2\mathbb{N}\cup\{0\}\Longrightarrow\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\sigma+m-\gamma,m}=\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m,\gamma,\sigma+m-\gamma}=0
.

Therefore, by setting
\sigma=2\tau
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3\ssomega_{m}^{2}}{8\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}+\frac{9\ssomega_{m}^{2}}{2\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\sum_{\tau=0}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\tau}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau}^{2}}+\frac{9\ssomega_{m}^{2}}{4\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\sum_{\tau=0}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\tau}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau}^{2}-(2\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma,m}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}+\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}-\frac{9}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m,2\tau,m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\tau}}{\ssomega_{2\tau}^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{9}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m,\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}.

Now, all the Fourier coefficients above are non–zero and, according to Lemma
5.9
, we have

\displaystyle\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\tau}

\displaystyle=2\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{(\tau+1)^{2}(\gamma-\tau+1)(\gamma+\tau+3)}{(\gamma+1)(\gamma+3)\sqrt{4\tau(\tau+2)+3}},

\displaystyle\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m,2\tau,m}

\displaystyle=2\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{(\tau+1)^{2}(m-\tau+1)(m+\tau+3)}{(m+1)(m+3)\sqrt{4\tau(\tau+2)+3}},

\displaystyle\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma,m}

\displaystyle=2\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{(\tau+1)(\gamma-\tau+1)(m+\tau+3)(-\gamma+m+\tau+1)}{\sqrt{(\gamma+1)(\gamma+3)(m+1)(m+3)(-\gamma+m+2\tau+1)(-\gamma+m+2\tau+3)}}.

The latter allows us to compute

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=0}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\tau}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\gamma+2)(2\gamma+3)(2\gamma+5)}{15\pi(\gamma+1)(\gamma+3)},

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m,2\tau,m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\tau}}{\ssomega_{2\tau}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\gamma+2)(-\gamma(\gamma+4)+5m(m+4)+15)}{15\pi(m+1)(m+3)},

for all integers
\gamma\geq 0
. Also, recall that
\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}
and
\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}
are given by closed formulas (Remark
5.11
). Consequently, we rescale
\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}
,

\displaystyle\frac{\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}}{\ssomega_{m}^{2}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{3}{8\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}+\frac{9}{2\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\sum_{\tau=0}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\tau}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau}^{2}}+\frac{9}{4\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\sum_{\tau=0}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\tau}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau}^{2}-(2\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}-\frac{9}{2\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma,m}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}+\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}-\frac{9}{4\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m,2\tau,m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\tau}}{\ssomega_{2\tau}^{2}}

\displaystyle-\frac{9}{2\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m,\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}

and obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}}{\ssomega_{m}^{2}}=\mathfrak{I}_{\gamma m}+\mathfrak{E}_{\gamma m},

(7.12)

where
\mathfrak{I}_{\gamma m}
stands for the part that can be explicitly computed,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{I}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle:=-\frac{3}{8\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}+\frac{9}{2\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\sum_{\tau=0}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\tau}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau}^{2}}+\frac{9}{4\ssomega_{\gamma}^{2}}\sum_{\tau=0}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\tau}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau}^{2}-(2\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{4\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}-\frac{9}{4\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\frac{\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m,2\tau,m}\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,\gamma,2\tau}}{\ssomega_{2\tau}^{2}},

and
\mathfrak{E}_{\gamma m}
stands for the part that cannot be explicitly computed,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}_{\gamma m}

\displaystyle:=-\frac{9}{2\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma,m}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}+\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}-\frac{9}{2\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m,\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma}\right)^{2}}{\ssomega_{2\tau+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}}.

Now, using the elementary inequalities

\displaystyle|\ssomega_{2\tau+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}+\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}|

\displaystyle=|4(\gamma-\tau+1)(m+\tau+3)|\geq 4(m+3),

\displaystyle|\ssomega_{2\tau+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}|

\displaystyle=|4(\tau+1)(-\gamma+m+\tau+1)|\geq 2(m+3),

for all
0\leq\tau\leq\gamma
and
\gamma\geq 0
, we estimate

\displaystyle\left|\mathfrak{E}_{\gamma m}\right|\leq\frac{9}{2\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma,m}\right)^{2}}{\left|\ssomega_{2\tau+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}+\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}\right|}+\frac{9}{2\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\frac{\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{m,\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma}\right)^{2}}{\left|\ssomega_{2\tau+m-\gamma}^{2}-(\ssomega_{m}-\ssomega_{\gamma})^{2}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{9}{2\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\frac{1}{4(m+3)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma,m}\right)^{2}+\frac{9}{2\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\frac{1}{2(m+3)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma,m}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{9}{2\ssomega_{m}^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{4(m+3)}+\frac{1}{2(m+3)}\right)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau=0\end{subarray}}^{\gamma}\left(\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma,m}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{9(\gamma+2)}{70\pi(m+1)(m+2)^{2}(m+3)^{2}}\Big[-3\gamma^{4}-24\gamma^{3}-40\gamma^{2}+32\gamma+7\gamma^{2}m^{2}+28\gamma m^{2}+35m^{2}

\displaystyle+28\gamma^{2}m+112\gamma m+140m+105\Big]:=\mathfrak{P}_{\gamma m},

where we used the closed formula for
\overline{\mathfrak{C}}_{\gamma,2\tau+m-\gamma,m}
from above. Hence, for all
m\geq 2\gamma+1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}}{\ssomega_{m}^{2}}=\mathfrak{I}_{\gamma m}+\mathfrak{E}_{\gamma}\geq\mathfrak{I}_{\gamma m}-\mathfrak{P}_{\gamma m}:=\mathfrak{O}_{\gamma m}.

(7.13)

Finally, for each
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
, we use the closed formulas for
\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma mm}
and
\mathfrak{C}_{\gamma\gamma\gamma\gamma}
we derived in Remark
5.11
to firstly explicitly compute
\mathfrak{I}_{\gamma m}
in terms of
m
and then explicitly compute
\mathfrak{O}_{\gamma m}
in terms of
m
. Once the closed formula is derived, we then show that
\mathfrak{O}_{\gamma m}>0
for all
m\geq 2\gamma+1
. For example, for
\gamma=0
, we find

\displaystyle\mathfrak{I}_{0m}=\frac{m(m+4)(5m(m+4)+29)+66}{12\pi(m+1)(m+2)^{2}(m+3)}

and hence

\displaystyle\mathfrak{O}_{0m}=\frac{5m^{4}+40m^{3}+109m^{2}+8m-42}{12\pi(m+1)(m+2)^{2}(m+3)},

that is greater than
10^{-3}
provided that
m\geq 1
. Similarly, for
\gamma=1
, we compute

\displaystyle\mathfrak{I}_{1m}=\frac{\left(m^{2}+2\right)(m(m+8)+18)}{4\pi(m+1)(m+2)^{2}(m+3)}

and hence

\displaystyle\mathfrak{O}_{1m}=\frac{m\left(m^{4}+11m^{3}+44m^{2}-32m-348\right)}{4\pi(m+1)(m+2)^{2}(m+3)^{2}},

that is greater than
10^{-3}
provided that
m\geq 3
.
For all the other cases with
\gamma\in\{2,3,4,5\}
, we find

\displaystyle\mathfrak{O}_{2m}

\displaystyle=\frac{109m^{5}+1199m^{4}+4523m^{3}-30347m^{2}-132936m+107244}{600\pi(m+1)(m+2)^{2}(m+3)^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{O}_{3m}

\displaystyle=\frac{43m^{5}+473m^{4}+1646m^{3}-33554m^{2}-129372m+238248}{300\pi(m+1)(m+2)^{2}(m+3)^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{O}_{4m}

\displaystyle=\frac{83m^{5}+913m^{4}+2851m^{3}-139159m^{2}-515982m+1611198}{700\pi(m+1)(m+2)^{2}(m+3)^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{O}_{5m}

\displaystyle=\frac{17m^{5}+187m^{4}+505m^{3}-53329m^{2}-194760m+905292}{168\pi(m+1)(m+2)^{2}(m+3)^{2}}

and the claim follows similarly
9
9

9

For
\gamma\in\{2,3\}
, the estimates
\mathfrak{O}_{\gamma m}\geq 10^{-3}
hold true for all integers
m\geq 2\gamma+1
, whereas, for
\gamma\in\{4,5\}
, we have that
\mathfrak{O}_{\gamma m}\geq 10^{-3}
provided that
m\geq 2\gamma+3
instead of
m\geq 2\gamma+1
. In this case, we use the definition of the Fourier coefficients to explicitly compute
\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma m}\ssomega_{m}^{-2}
for
m\in\{2\gamma+1,2\gamma+2\}
and verify that it is still strictly positive.
. Finally, it remains to show that the determinants

\displaystyle\mathfrak{D}_{\gamma n}

\displaystyle:=\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma n}\mathfrak{u}_{\gamma,2\gamma-n}-\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma n}\mathfrak{v}_{\gamma,2\gamma-n}

are all non–zero for
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
that follows by a direct computation using the definition of the Fourier coefficients. Specifically, we compute
\mathfrak{D}_{\gamma n}
for all
\gamma\in\{0,1,2,3,4,5\}
and
n\in\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
and verify that they are all strictly negative, that completes the proof. Figure

\mathfrak{D}_{\gamma n}
for
\gamma=5
(blue),

(orange) and
\gamma=7
(green) as
n
varies within
\{0,1,\dots,\gamma-1\}
. They are all in fact non–zero.

illustrates the determinants
\mathfrak{D}_{\gamma n}
for
\gamma\in\{5,6,7\}
as

.
